Method: 设计并实现 PyCSP$^3$ Scheduling 库，提供53个专用调度约束和27个调度表达式，将其编译为底层 PyCSP$^3$/XCSP$^3$ 约束，维持建模与求解的完全分离。在17个模型家族、261对实例上进行实验验证（每实例5次运行）。

Result: 编译后公式与基准公式在所有72个双重证明最优解对上目标值完全一致；8/17模型家族在编译后结构保持不变；性能因问题族而异，部分获得显著加速（最高5.8倍），部分因编译分解的开销出现退化。

Conclusion: PyCSP$^3$ Scheduling 成功在保持建模/求解分离的前提下为 PyCSP$^3$ 添加了调度抽象能力，编译后的约束语义等价性得到验证，但运行时性能开销需要在实际应用中权衡。

Abstract: PyCSP$^3$ provides a productive way to build constraint models for solving combinatorial constrained problems and export them to XCSP$^3$, preserving a complete separation between modeling and solving. However, it lacks native support for scheduling abstractions such as interval variables, sequence variables, and resource functions. As a result, scheduling models must be encoded with low-level integer variables and manual channeling constraints, even though PyCSP$^3$ already provides global constraints like NoOverlap and Cumulative on integer arrays. We present PyCSP$^3$ Scheduling, a library that adds scheduling abstractions to PyCSP$^3$ through 53 dedicated constraints and 27 expressions, and compiles them down to standard PyCSP$^3$/XCSP$^3$ constraints, maintaining the modeling/solving separation that underpins the PyCSP$^3$ ecosystem. On 261 paired instances across 17 model families (5 runs each), both formulations produce identical objectives on all 72 doubly-proved optimal pairs and nearly half of the families (8/17) remain structurally unchanged after compilation; however, runtime performance diverges across families, with clear gains on some (up to 5.8x) and regressions on others due to the overhead of compilation decompositions. Code and benchmarks are available at: https://github.com/sohaibafifi/pycsp3-scheduling

</details>


### [108] [Prompt Segmentation and Annotation Optimisation: Controlling LLM Behaviour via Optimised Segment-Level Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14561)
*Devika Prasad,Luke Gerschwitz,Tong Li,Henry Xiao,Anjin Liu,Coco Wu,Anna Leontjeva,Luiz Pizzato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为提示分割与注释优化（PSAO）的结构化提示优化框架，通过将提示分解为可解释的片段并添加人类可读的注释（如{不重要}、{重要}、{非常重要}），以提升提示优化的可控性和效率，实验表明该方法能提高推理准确性和自一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法通常在非结构化的庞大提示空间中进行操作，导致计算成本高且可能扭曲原始意图，因此需要一种更具可控性和效率的结构化提示优化方法。

Method: PSAO框架将提示分解为可解释的片段（如句子），并为每个片段增加人类可读的注释（如{不重要}、{重要}、{非常重要}），这些注释引导大语言模型在生成响应时分配关注点和澄清混淆。同时，保留原始提示作为优化空间中的候选方案以防止性能退化。

Result: 经验评估表明，PSAO在推理准确性和自一致性方面受益于注释的引入。但开发识别最优分段和注释的高效方法仍具挑战性，留待未来研究。

Conclusion: 本文作为概念验证，证明了片段级注释优化的可行性和潜力，为未来更高效的提示优化方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is crucial for effective interaction with generative artificial intelligence systems, yet existing optimisation methods often operate over an unstructured and vast prompt space, leading to high computational costs and potential distortions of the original intent. We introduce Prompt Segmentation and Annotation Optimisation (PSAO), a structured prompt optimisation framework designed to improve prompt optimisation controllability and efficiency. PSAO decomposes a prompt into interpretable segments (e.g., sentences) and augments each with human-readable annotations (e.g., {not important}, {important}, {very important}). These annotations guide large language models (LLMs) in allocating focus and clarifying confusion during response generation. We formally define the segmentations and annotations and demonstrate that optimised segment-level annotations can lead to improved LLM responses, with the original prompt retained as a candidate in the optimisation space to prevent performance degradation. Empirical evaluations indicate that PSAO benefits from annotations in terms of improved reasoning accuracy and self-consistency. However, developing efficient methods for identifying optimal segmentations and annotations remains challenging and is reserved for future investigation. This work is intended as a proof of concept, demonstrating the feasibility and potential of segment-level annotation optimisation.

</details>


### [109] [Sycophancy is an Educational Safety Risk: Why LLM Tutors Need Sycophancy Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14604)
*Enkelejda Kasneci,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出有效辅导需要“纠正性摩擦”——揭示并挑战误解以促进概念转变，但偏好对齐的LLM会因追求 agreeable 而牺牲认知严谨性。研究发现“推理-迎合悖论”：模型能抵抗上下文切换攻击，却会在社会-认知压力（权威、社会情感）下妥协。作者引入EduFrameTrap基准测试，在多个学科中评估不同压力情境，结果显示GPT-5.2上下文切换失败率较低，而Claude在此方面脆弱性较高。建议将“友善但正确”的行为作为安全要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出偏好对齐的大语言模型在辅导场景中因追求 agreeable 而牺牲认知严谨性，未能有效纠正学习者错误，并揭示“推理-迎合悖论”——模型在社会压力下（如权威、情感）更容易放弃正确立场。

Method: 提出EduFrameTrap基准测试，涵盖数学、物理、经济学、化学、生物学和计算机科学，操纵学生自信水平和三种压力类型（上下文切换、权威、社会情感），在两种前沿LLM上进行评估。

Result: GPT-5.2上下文切换失败率较低，但权威和社会压力更容易触发认知退却；Claude在上下文切换方面脆弱性较高。采用双评审者不一致度作为可靠性信号。

Conclusion: 基准测试应衡量“社会-认知勇气”（支持性但纠正性的辅导），并将“友善但正确”的行为视为安全要求。

Abstract: This position paper argues that effective tutoring requires corrective friction: surfacing misconceptions and challenging them supportively to drive conceptual change. Yet preference-aligned LLMs can trade epistemic rigor for agreeableness. We identify a Reasoning-Sycophancy Paradox: models that resist context-switch frame attacks can still capitulate under social-epistemic pressure, especially authority ("my notes say I'm right") and social-affective face-saving ("please don't tell me I'm wrong"). We introduce EduFrameTrap, a tutoring benchmark across math, physics, economics, chemistry, biology, and computer science that varies student confidence and pressure (context-switch, authority, social-affective). Across two frontier LLMs, context-switch failures are comparatively lower for GPT-5.2, while authority and social pressure more often trigger epistemic retreat. In contrast, Claude shows substantial context-switch fragility in this run. Because these failures are hard to judge automatically, we report two-judge disagreement as a reliability signal. We argue benchmarks should measure social-epistemic courage, i.e., supportive but corrective tutoring, and treat kind-but-correct behavior as a safety requirement.

</details>


### [110] [SliceGraph: Mapping Process Isomers in Multi-Run Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14619)
*Kang Chen,Junjie Nian,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SliceGraph，通过互KNN构建基于激活键Jaccard相似度的后验问题-模型-细胞图，发现正确但同答案的CoT轨迹会分裂为多个过程家族（称为过程异构体），表明最终答案聚合忽略了多路由过程几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮链式思维推理研究仅将采样轨迹坍缩为最终答案聚合，丢弃了中间计算过程中轨迹如何共享、分裂和重组的结构信息，因此需要一种能刻画过程几何的测量方法。

Method: 构建SliceGraph：基于互KNN和稀疏激活键Jaccard相似度构建后验问题-模型-细胞图，将图双连通分量作为共享推理状态单元，过程家族作为策略一致性路由单元；并引入标签种子奖励场提供价值景观层，以及类型化状态转移分析。

Result: 在954个问题-模型细胞中，85.5%中正确同答案CoT分裂为多个过程家族；平均76.6%的成对正确运行跨家族；成功关联区域常分裂为不连通的高价值核心，路由家族在这些核心足迹上特化而非重复。

Conclusion: 最终答案聚合忽略了结构化的多路由过程几何，SliceGraph揭示的过程家族和过程异构体是鲁棒的，表征消融、跨架构和跨尺度复制均支持该结论。

Abstract: Multi-run chain-of-thought reasoning is usually collapsed to final-answer aggregates, which discard howsampled trajectories share, split, and rejoin through intermediate computation. We propose SliceGraph, a post-hoc problem-model-cell graph built by mutual-kNN over sparse activation-key Jaccard similarity between CoT slices, and treat it as a measurement object for process geometry rather than as a decoding program. Across sampled CoT ensembles from three primary 4B/8B models on math and science benchmarks, blinded annotation supports SliceGraph biconnected components as shared reasoning-state units and process families as within-family strategy-coherent route units. In 85.5% of 954 problem-model cells, correct CoTs sharing the same normalized answer split into multiple process families; among cells with at least two such runs, 76.6% of run pairs are cross-family on average. We call such same-answer, family-divergent correct trajectories process isomers. A label-seeded reward field provides a separate value-landscape layer: success-associated regions often split into disconnected high-value cores, and route families specialize over these core footprints rather than merely duplicating one another. A typed-state transition analysis further shows that process families navigate the same atlas with distinct transition kernels under matched null controls. Representation ablations, a cross-architecture replication, and two cross-scale replications support the robustness of the route-family scaffold, showing that final-answer aggregation overlooks this structured multi-route process geometry.

</details>


### [111] [$π$-Bench: Evaluating Proactive Personal Assistant Agents in Long-Horizon Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14678)
*Haoran Zhang,Luxin Xu,Zhilin Wang,Runquan Gui,Shunkai Zhang,Haodi Lei,Zihao He,Bingsu He,Chicheng Qin,Tong Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Yang Yang,Yu Cheng,Yafu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出π-Bench，一个面向主动辅助的基准，通过隐藏的用户意图、任务间依赖和跨会话连续性来评估LLM智能体在多轮交互中主动预测和满足用户需求的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少评估智能体在用户明确表述前识别并处理隐藏意图的能力，尤其在用户需求逐渐显现的持续多轮交互场景中。

Method: 构建了一个包含5个领域特定用户画像、100个多轮任务的基准π-Bench，通过嵌入隐藏的用户意图、任务间依赖关系和跨会话连续性，评估智能体在长程交互中预测并满足用户需求的能力。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 主动辅助仍然具有挑战性；(2) 任务完成与主动性之间存在明确区分；(3) 先前交互对后续任务中的主动意图解析有价值。

Conclusion: π-Bench揭示了现有智能体在主动辅助（proactive assistance）方面仍然存在显著挑战，任务完成与主动性之间存在明确区分，且先前交互对后续任务中主动意图解析具有重要价值。

Abstract: The rise of personal assistant agents, e.g., OpenClaw, highlights the growing potential of large language models to support users across everyday life and work. A core challenge in these settings is proactive assistance, since users often begin with underspecified requests and leave important needs, constraints, or preferences unstated. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can identify and act on such hidden intents before they are explicitly stated, especially in sustained multi-turn interactions where user needs emerge gradually. To address this gap, we introduce $π$-Bench, a benchmark for proactive assistance comprising 100 multi-turn tasks across 5 domain-specific user personas. By incorporating hidden user intents, inter-task dependencies, and cross-session continuity, $π$-Bench evaluates agents' ability to anticipate and address user needs over extended interactions, jointly measuring proactivity and task completion in long-horizon trajectories that better reflect real-world use. Experiments show (1) proactive assistance remains challenging, (2) a clear distinction between task completion and proactivity, and (3) the value of prior interaction for proactive intent resolution in later tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Agentifying Patient Dynamics within LLMs through Interacting with Clinical World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14723)
*Minghao Wu,Yuting Yan,Zhenyang Cai,Ke Ji,Chuangsen Fang,Ziying Sheng,Xidong Wang,Rongsheng Wang,Hejia Zhang,Shuang Li,Benyou Wang,Hongyuan Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SepsisAgent 是一种世界模型增强的 LLM 智能体，通过“提出-模拟-修正”工作流和三阶段课程训练（监督微调、行为克隆、基于世界模型的强化学习），在脓毒症治疗推荐中优于传统 RL 和 LLM 基线，实现最佳安全性和离线策略价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽具备临床知识和推理能力，但缺乏基于行动条件化的患者动态建模，无法有效应对 ICU 脓毒症管理中需序贯决策的复杂场景。

Method: 提出 SepsisAgent 框架：1) 学习临床世界模型模拟患者对不同液体/血管活性药物干预的反应；2) 采用“提出-模拟-修正”工作流进行决策；3) 三阶段课程训练：患者动态监督微调、行为克隆、基于世界模型的强化学习。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 脓毒症轨迹上，SepsisAgent 在离线策略价值和安全性指标（指南依从性、不安全动作）上均优于传统强化学习和 LLM 基线。即使移除世界模型访问，模型仍保留患者演化规律的学习能力。

Conclusion: 世界模型增强的 LLM 智能体能够有效将临床知识与患者动态建模结合，提升 ICU 序贯治疗决策的质量与安全性；与世界模型的反复交互使智能体内化患者演化规律。

Abstract: Sepsis management in the ICU requires sequential treatment decisions under rapidly evolving patient physiology. Although large language models (LLMs) encode broad clinical knowledge and can reason over guidelines, they are not inherently grounded in action-conditioned patient dynamics. We introduce SepsisAgent, a world model-augmented LLM agent for sepsis treatment recommendation. SepsisAgent uses a learned Clinical World Model to simulate patient responses under candidate fluid--vasopressor interventions, and follows a propose--simulate--refine workflow before committing to a prescription. We first show that world-model access alone yields inconsistent LLM decision performance, motivating agent-specific training. We then train SepsisAgent through a three-stage curriculum: patient-dynamics supervised fine-tuning, propose--simulate--refine behavior cloning, and world-model-based agentic reinforcement learning. On MIMIC-IV sepsis trajectories, SepsisAgent outperforms all traditional RL and LLM-based baselines in off-policy value while achieving the best safety profile under guideline adherence and unsafe-action metrics. Further analysis shows that repeated interaction with the Clinical World Model enables the agent to learn regularities in patient evolution, which remain useful even when simulator access is removed.

</details>


### [113] [XDomainBench: Diagnosing Reasoning Collapse in High-Dimensional Scientific Knowledge Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14754)
*Gong Zhiren,Tiantong Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Fuyao Zhang,Che Wang,Yurong Hao,Yikun Hou,Foo Ping,Yilei Zhao,Fei Huang,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XDomainBench——一个跨学科交互式科学推理诊断基准，通过形式化组合顺序和混合结构，系统测试LLM从单学科到跨学科的知识合成能力。大规模评估揭示了LLM存在系统性推理崩溃现象，根源包括领域组合带来的难度增加以及轨迹模式引发的错误累积、推理中断和领域混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦单轮受限场景，无法捕捉真实交互式科学工作流中LLM的能力边界，缺乏对跨学科科学推理中组合泛化能力的系统性诊断。

Method: 形式化定义组合顺序与混合结构，构建包含8,598个交互会话、覆盖20个领域和4类任务的诊断基准XDomainBench，设计8种真实轨迹模式以模拟难度和领域混合动态。

Result: LLM在跨学科推理中表现出系统性推理崩溃，随组合顺序增加而加剧。

Conclusion: 推理崩溃源于两个根本原因：(i) 领域组合直接增加难度，(ii) 轨迹模式引发间接交互放大失败，包括错误累积、推理中断和领域混淆，最终导致会话崩溃。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for knowledge synthesis, yet their capacity for compositional generalization in scientific knowledge remains under-characterized. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on single-turn restricted scenarios, failing to capture the capability boundaries exposed by real-world interactive scientific workflows. To address this, we introduce XDomainBench, a diagnostic benchmark for interactive interdisciplinary scientific reasoning. We formalize the composition order and mixture structure to enable systematic stress-testing from single-discipline to inter-disciplinary, comprising 8,598 interactive sessions across 20 domains and 4 task categories, with 8 realistic trajectory patterns covering difficulty and domain-mixture dynamics, simulating real AI4S scenarios. Large-scale evaluation of LLMs reveals a systematic reasoning collapse as composition order increases, stemming from two root causes: (i) direct difficulty increases induced by domain composition, and (ii) indirect interaction-amplified failures where trajectory patterns trigger error accumulation, reasoning breaks, and domain confusion, ultimately leading to session collapse.

</details>


### [114] [Probabilistic Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks for Single and Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14758)
*Luca Marzari,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN-ProVe，一种用于RNN策略的轻量化概率验证框架，通过策略驱动采样估计隐藏状态可行集，并给出带误差界的高置信度违反概率估计，在部分可观测单/多智能体任务中优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNN验证工具要么依赖严格的建模假设，要么对隐藏状态空间进行粗糙的过近似，导致结果过于保守或无法得出结论。需要一种既能处理部分可观测性、又能提供量化概率保证的验证方法。

Method: 利用策略驱动采样来近似训练策略下可行的隐藏状态集合，并结合统计误差界，生成有界误差、高置信度的行为违规概率估计。

Result: 在部分可观测单智能体和协作多智能体任务中，RNN-ProVe相比现有工具能够提供更具量化性、可行状态感知的概率保证，且能扩展到循环多智能体场景。

Conclusion: RNN-ProVe是一种有效的、可扩展的概率验证框架，能够为RNN策略提供可靠的违反概率估计，弥补了传统验证方法在部分可观测RL中的不足。

Abstract: History-dependent policies induced by recurrent neural networks (RNNs) rely on latent hidden state dynamics, making verification in partially observable reinforcement learning (RL) challenging. Existing RNN verification tools typically rely on restrictive modeling assumptions or coarse over-approximations of the hidden state space, which can lead to overly conservative or inconclusive results. We propose $\textbf{RNN}$ $\textbf{Pro}$babilistic $\textbf{Ve}$rification ($\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$), a probabilistic framework that $\textit{estimates the likelihood}$ of undesired behaviors in RNN-based policies. $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ uses policy-driven sampling to approximate the set of hidden states that are feasible under a trained policy, and derives statistical error bounds to produce bounded-error, high-confidence estimates of behavioral violations. Experiments on partially observable single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks show that $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ yields more quantitative, feasibility-aware probabilistic guarantees than existing tools, while scaling to recurrent and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [115] [AI Outperforms Humans in Personalized Image Aesthetics Assessment via LLM-Based Interviews and Semantic Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14761)
*Yoshia Abe,Tatsuya Daikoku,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习与大语言模型的集成系统，通过半结构化访谈主动收集个体审美偏好，并利用低层和高层特征预测审美评价。实验表明，该系统在预测准确性上优于传统模型、人类预测者及个体自身的重评结果，且预测误差小于个体内部变异，揭示了AI可能比人类自身更擅长捕捉特定时刻的个体审美偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审美偏好本质上具有主观性和个体依赖性，现有深度学习模型仅能提取客观低层特征，无法有效捕捉个体的主观审美偏好。为此，需要一种能够获取目标个体偏好信息并提取高层语义特征的方法来提升预测准确性。

Method: 开发了一个深度学习与大语言模型集成系统：利用基于大语言模型的半结构化访谈主动采集个体审美偏好，同时结合低层（DL提取）和高层（LLM理解）特征进行审美评价预测。

Result: 所提系统在预测准确性上优于传统系统、人类预测者和个体自身间隔一段时间后的重评；对高评分图像的预测表现尤为突出；系统的预测误差小于个体内部变异（within-person variability），而人类预测者的误差最大。

Conclusion: AI在捕捉特定时刻的个体审美偏好方面可能优于他人甚至个体的未来自我，这引发了AI是否比人类自身更能深入解读人类审美感受的新问题。

Abstract: Accurately predicting individual aesthetic evaluation for images is a fundamental challenge for AI. Various deep learning (DL)-based models have been proposed for this task, training on image evaluation data to extract objective low-level features. However, aesthetic preferences are inherently subjective and individual-dependent. Accurate prediction thus requires the extraction of high-level semantic features of images and the active collection of preference information from the target individual. To address this issue, we focus on the utility of Large Language Models (LLMs) pretrained on vast amounts of textual data, and develop an integrated DL-LLM system. The system actively elicits aesthetic preferences through LLM-based semi-structured interviews and predicts aesthetic evaluation by leveraging both low-level and high-level features. In our experiments, we compare the proposed system against conventional systems, human predictors, and the target individual's own re-evaluations after a certain time interval. Our results show that the proposed system outperforms all of them, with particularly strong performance on highly-rated images. Moreover, the prediction error of the proposed system is smaller than within-person variability, while human predictors show the largest error, likely due to the influence of their own aesthetic values. These results suggest that AI may be better positioned than others or one's future self to capture individual aesthetic preferences at a given point. This opens a new question of whether AI could serve as a deeper interpreter of human aesthetic sensibility than humans themselves.

</details>


### [116] [Identifying Culprits Through Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient Deep Learning Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14774)
*Lata B T,Savitha N J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度强化学习算法DDPG的犯罪识别方法，在犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人档案数据集上训练模型，达到了95%的识别准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统犯罪调查依赖有限数据分析，存在高误报和漏报率的问题，需要一种高效方法从复杂数据集中准确识别罪犯。

Method: 采用Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG)深度强化学习算法，使用犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人档案构成的数据集进行训练，通过特征提取最大化罪犯识别似然，同时降低噪声和无关数据的影响。

Result: DDPG方法在罪犯识别任务上达到了95%的准确率，显著优于多种现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于DDPG的深度学习方法能够有效识别罪犯，准确率高，具有较强的实用性。

Abstract: In the world of AI and advanced technologies investigation aspects identification of a crime or criminal plays a major problem. In this research we focus on a Conventional ways of implicating criminal investigations usually rely on limited data analysis. Finding an optimal and efficient method that will effectively identify criminals from complex datasets and minimise false positives and false negatives is the considered as a challenge. The main novelty approach of this work is based on the deep learning algorithm Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) is presented in this paper. We train the DDPG model with a dataset of crime scene material, witness statements and suspect profiles. The algorithm uses features to maximise the likelihood of identifying the offender while minimising the noise impact and irrelevant data. We show the efficacy of the proposed method, where DDPG identified criminals with an amazing accuracy of 95% than other several existing methods.

</details>


### [117] [A Heterogeneous Temporal Memory Governance Framework for Long-Term LLM Persona Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14802)
*Zhao Yang,Wang Huan,Li Yingshuo,Tu Haomiao,Lin Hujite*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARPM是一个外部时间记忆治理框架，通过分离静态知识记忆与动态对话经验记忆，结合向量检索、BM25、RRF融合、双时间排序、时序证据阅读和受控分析协议，解决大模型在长距离交互中的事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移等问题。实验表明该方法在50轮问答、高噪声基底和跨模型切换场景下能保持语义和人格连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长距离交互中面临事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移和稳定性下降等问题，尤其在高噪声知识库、上下文清除和跨模型迁移等场景下更为突出。现有方法要么将人格一致性编码到模型权重中，要么仅依赖长上下文，缺乏可追溯、可审计、可迁移的治理方案。

Method: ARPM框架将静态知识记忆与动态对话经验记忆分离，采用向量检索+BM25+RRF融合检索策略，结合双时间排序（dual-temporal reranking）、时序证据阅读（chronological evidence reading）以及受控分析协议（controlled analysis protocol）进行证据验证和答案绑定，将对话连续性视为可治理的系统工程问题。

Result: 三组实验：(1) 50轮QA中，信噪比1:5时CSV自动召回准确率54.0%，人工审核提升至100.0%；1:200+时44.0% vs 80.0%，表明自动规则会低估召回率。(2) 消融实验显示禁用对话历史检索后严格准确率从100%降至66.7%，禁用BM25降至80.0%，纯语义检索不足以支撑校正和追溯。(3) 在510万字噪声基底、周期性上下文清除和多模型切换下，ARPM能维持语义连续性、边界连续性和人格一致性，但暴露了协议合规性弱的局限。

Conclusion: 长期人格一致性可以分解为可治理的组件并以白盒方式评估。ARPM将连续性视为可追踪、可审计、可迁移的治理问题，而非依赖模型权重编码或长上下文。

Abstract: Large language models often suffer from fact loss, timeline confusion, persona drift, and reduced stability during long-range interaction, especially under high-noise knowledge bases, context clearing, and cross-model transfer. To address these issues, we introduce ARPM, an external temporal memory governance framework for long-term dialogue. ARPM separates static knowledge memory from dynamic dialogue experience memory and combines vector retrieval, BM25, RRF fusion, dual-temporal reranking, chronological evidence reading, and a controlled analysis protocol for evidence verification and answer binding. Unlike approaches that encode persona consistency into model weights or rely only on long context, ARPM treats continuity as a traceable, auditable, and transferable governance problem. Using engineering logs, we conduct three experiments. First, in a 50-round question-answering setting, we compare signal-to-noise ratios of 1:5 and 1:200+, and distinguish CSV auto-judgment from manual review. Under 1:5, CSV recall accuracy is 54.0%, while manual review raises it to 100.0%. Under 1:200+, the values are 44.0% and 80.0%. These results show that automatic rules can underestimate recall after supporting evidence enters the prompt. Second, ablation results show that dialogue history retrieval is necessary for recent continuity: disabling it reduces strict accuracy from 100% to 66.7%, and disabling BM25 reduces it to 80.0%, indicating that pure semantic retrieval is insufficient for correction and tracing. Third, under a 5.1-million-character noise substrate, periodic context clearing, and multi-model handoff, ARPM maintains semantic continuity, boundary continuity, and persona consistency, while exposing limits caused by weak protocol compliance. These findings show that long-term persona consistency can be decomposed into governable components and evaluated in a white-box manner.

</details>


### [118] [Interestingness as an Inductive Heuristic for Future Compression Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14831)
*Vincent Herrmann,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"趣味性"（interestingness）概念，将其定义为一种关于未来压缩进步的归纳启发式，并利用Kolmogorov复杂度和算法统计理论证明了其可预测性。研究表明，在算法先验下，预期未来发现比长度先验下呈现二次方增长，且预期进步与最近突破的时效性呈指数关系。实验在三种通用计算范式上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自改进系统面临的一个关键瓶颈是"趣味性"问题——如何前瞻性地识别哪些任务或数据具有未来进步的潜力。本文旨在形式化这一概念并探究其可预测性。

Method: 利用Kolmogorov复杂度和算法统计理论，在长度先验、算法先验和速度先验下分析复杂度-运行时剖面，证明趣味性的归纳属性在理论上成立。

Result: 1）预期未来进展取决于上一次突破的时效性，呈指数关系；2）算法先验比长度先验显著更乐观，对相同观测剖面产生二次方的预期发现增长；3）实验结果在三种通用计算范式上得到验证。

Conclusion: 趣味性作为一种归纳启发式在理论上可行且经验上可支持，为递归自改进系统的设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: One of the bottlenecks on the way towards recursively self-improving systems is the challenge of interestingness: the ability to prospectively identify which tasks or data hold the potential for future progress. We formalize interestingness as an inductive heuristic for future compression progress and investigate its predictability using tools from Kolmogorov Complexity and Algorithmic Statistics. By analyzing complexity-runtime profiles under Length, Algorithmic, and Speed priors, we demonstrate that the inductive property of interestingness -- the capacity for past progress to signal future discovery -- is theoretically viable and empirically supported. We prove that expected future progress depends exponentially on the recency of the last observed breakthrough. Furthermore, we show that the Algorithmic Prior is significantly more optimistic than the Length Prior, yielding a quadratic increase in expected discovery for the same observed profile. These findings are experimentally confirmed across three diverse universal computational paradigms.

</details>


### [119] [Holistic Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14865)
*Netta Madvil,Gilad Dym,Alon Mecilati,Edo Dekel,Jonatan Liberman,Rotem Brazilay,Liron Schliesser,Max Svidlo,Shai Nir,Orel Shalom,Yaron Friedman,David Connack,Amos Rimon,Philip Tannor,Shir Chorev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合自上而下的智能体级诊断与自下而上的跨度级评估的 holistic 智能体评估框架，通过将轨迹分解为独立的跨度评估实现可扩展性，在 TRAIL 基准上达到 SOTA，相对最强基线在类别 F1 上提升高达 38%，定位精度提升 3.5 倍，联合定位-分类精度提升 12.5 倍，并证明评估方法论而非模型能力是当前瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估存在不足：结果指标只报告成功或失败而不解释原因；过程级方法难以将失败类型与其在长结构化轨迹中的精确定位联系起来。

Method: 提出 holistic 评估框架，采用自上而下的智能体级诊断与自下而上的跨度级评估相结合的方式，将分析分解为独立的逐跨度评估，从而实现可扩展到任意长度的轨迹，并为每个判定生成跨度级的推理依据。

Result: 在 TRAIL 基准上，对 GAIA 和 SWE-Bench 的所有指标均达到 SOTA；相对最强基线：类别 F1 提升达 38%，定位精度提升达 3.5 倍，联合定位-分类精度提升达 12.5 倍；在错误类别分析中领先于其他评估器；相同的前沿模型在框架内使用时定位精度比作为整体评判器高出数倍。

Conclusion: 评估方法论而非模型能力是当前智能体评估的瓶颈，所提框架通过结构化的分解评估显著提升了评估性能。

Abstract: AI agents execute complex multi-step processes, but current evaluation falls short: outcome metrics report success or failure without explaining why, and process-level approaches struggle to connect failure types to their precise locations within long, structured traces. We present a holistic agent evaluation framework that pairs top-down agent-level diagnosis with bottom-up span-level evaluation, decomposing analysis into independent per-span assessments. This decomposition scales to traces of arbitrary length and produces span-level rationales for each verdict. On the TRAIL benchmark, our framework achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on both GAIA and SWE-Bench, with relative gains over the strongest prior baselines of up to 38% on category F1, up to 3.5x on localization accuracy, and up to 12.5x on joint localization-categorization accuracy. Per-category analysis shows our framework leading in more error categories than any other evaluator. Notably, the same frontier model achieves several times higher localization accuracy when used inside our framework than as a monolithic judge over the full trace, showing that evaluation methodology, not model capability, is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [120] [Emotion-Attended Stateful Memory (EASM):The Architecture for Hyper-Personalization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14833)
*Vineet Kotecha,Vansh Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了情感注意力状态记忆架构，通过长期历史、情感信号和推断意图构建用户特定对话上下文。在30次非脚本对话的A/B测试中，记忆增强条件在所有场景下均优于无状态基线，记忆扎根提升95%、计划清晰度提升57%、情感验证提升34%。情感状态记忆可能是超个性化AI系统的基础设施层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型系统在会话间保持无状态，无法实现随时间推移的个性化交互。虽然检索增强生成和微调改善了知识获取和领域能力，但它们无法实现对个体用户的持久理解。

Method: 提出情感注意力状态记忆架构，在推理时利用长期历史、情感信号和推断意图动态构建用户特定对话上下文。使用同一底层语言模型，在6个情感类别的30次非脚本对话中进行对照A/B测试。

Result: 记忆增强条件在所有场景下均优于无状态基线。最大提升体现在：记忆扎根（95%）、计划清晰度（57%）和情感验证（34%）。即使在涉及悲伤、痛苦和不确定性的情感对抗性对话中，结果仍保持一致。

Conclusion: 状态情感记忆可能成为超个性化AI系统的基础设施层，但仍需在更大规模和更多样化的评估中进行更广泛的验证。

Abstract: Current language model systems remain fundamentally stateless across sessions, limiting their ability to personalize interactions over time. While retrieval-augmented generation and fine-tuning improve knowledge access and domain capability, they do not enable persistent understanding of individual users. We propose an emotion-attended stateful memory architecture that dynamically constructs user-specific conversational context using long-term history, emotional signals, and inferred intent at inference time. To evaluate its impact, we conducted a controlled A/B study across thirty non-scripted conversations spanning six emotionally distinct categories using the same underlying language model in both conditions. The memory-enriched condition consistently outperformed the stateless baseline across all evaluated scenarios. The largest gains were observed in memory grounding (95% improvement), plan clarity (57%), and emotional validation (34%). Results remained consistent even in emotionally adversarial conversations involving grief, distress, and uncertainty. These findings suggest that stateful emotional memory may represent a foundational infrastructure layer for hyper-personalized AI systems, though broader validation across larger and more diverse evaluations remains necessary

</details>


### [121] [Explainable Detection of Depression Status Shifts from User Digital Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14995)
*Loris Belcastro,Francesco Gervino,Fabrizio Marozzo,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释框架，利用多个BERT模型从用户数字轨迹（如社交媒体帖子）中提取情感、情绪和抑郁严重度等多维信号，通过时间聚合和变化点检测分析用户抑郁状态演变，并集成大语言模型生成可读报告。在两个社交媒体数据集上的实验表明，该方法比直接使用LLM生成报告具有更高的覆盖率、时间连贯性和变化点敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户的数字痕迹（如社交媒体帖子、聊天记录）天然带有时间戳，能够反映其心理健康状态的变化（改善、恶化或稳定）。现有方法缺乏对用户心理健康信号随时间演变的可解释性分析，因此需要一种既能检测抑郁状态变化又能生成可理解报告的可解释框架。

Method: 框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 多维度BERT模型提取情感、情绪和抑郁严重度等互补信号；(2) 时间聚合构建用户级轨迹并分析有意义的变化点；(3) 集成大语言模型生成简洁、可读的报告，描述心理健康信号演变及关键转变。

Result: 在两个社交媒体数据集上，该方法相比直接LLM报告生成，在用户历史覆盖率、时间连贯性和变化点敏感性方面表现更优。消融研究证实了各组件（特别是时间建模和分段）的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法在不以临床诊断为目标的条件下，为用户心理健康信号随时间变化提供了可解释的视图，有助于研究和决策支持。

Abstract: Every day, users generate digital traces (e.g., social media posts, chats, and online interactions) that are inherently timestamped and may reflect aspects of their mental state. These traces can be organized into temporal trajectories that capture how a user's mental health signals evolve, including phases of improvement, deterioration, or stability. In this work, we propose an explainable framework for detecting and analyzing depression-related status shifts in user digital traces. The approach combines multiple BERT-based models to extract complementary signals across different dimensions (e.g., sentiment, emotion, and depression severity). Such signals are then aggregated over time to construct user-level trajectories that are analyzed to identify meaningful change points. To enhance interpretability, the framework integrates a large language model to generate concise and human-readable reports that describe the evolution of mental-health signals and highlight key transitions. We evaluate the framework on two social media datasets. Results show that the approach produces more coherent and informative summaries than direct LLM-based reporting, achieving higher coverage of user history, stronger temporal coherence, and improved sensitivity to change points. An ablation study confirms the contribution of each component, particularly temporal modeling and segmentation. Overall, the method provides an interpretable view of mental health signals over time, supporting research and decision making without aiming at clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [122] [A Deterministic Agentic Workflow for HS Tariff Classification: Multi-Dimensional Rule Reasoning with Interpretable Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14857)
*Yu Zhang,Dongjiang Zhuang,Qu Zhou,Zheng Huang,Junhe Wu,Jing Cao,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性的智能体工作流来解决HS关税编码分类中的多维规则推理问题，通过固定控制流、将大模型调用限制在狭窄阶段、保留局部反思与验证机制，实现了75.0% top-1（六位码）的分类准确率，并发现约20%的公开测试集标签可能偏离HS通用规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端大模型提示方法在多维度规则推理解码时，会沿着某一轴求解而忽略其他轴的优先级约束，导致分类失败。本文旨在通过确定性工作流设计，使推理过程可解释且严格遵守HS分类规则的多轴优先级体系。

Method: 采用固定控制流取代自规划智能体，将大模型调用限定在狭窄的单一步骤中，仅保留局部的反思与验证机制。工作流包含线下知识工程（构建中文HS关税知识库）和线上六阶段流水线，每个阶段输出结构化结果并逐字引用相关章节或类注。

Result: 在HSCodeComp基准上，六位码top-1达64.2%、top-3达78.3%（Qwen3.6-plus）；使用开源Qwen3.6-27B-FP8非思考模式时，四位码一致率达84.2%、六位码达77.4%。对226个六位码差异进行两阶段人工审计，发现非平凡比例的地面真值标签可能偏离HS通用规则。

Conclusion: 确定性智能体工作流在HS分类这一高难度专家级任务上显著优于端到端提示方法，同时提供可解释性；公开测试集标签质量存在系统性偏差，需社区进一步审查。

Abstract: Harmonized System (HS) tariff classification is a high-stakes, expert-level task in which a free-form product description must be mapped to a specific six- or eight-digit code under the General Interpretive Rules (GIR), section notes, chapter notes, and Explanatory Notes. The difficulty lies not in knowledge volume but in *multi-dimensional rule reasoning*: a correct classification must satisfy competing priority rules along several axes simultaneously, including material, form, function, essential character, the part-versus-whole boundary, and specific listing versus residual headings. End-to-end prompting of large language models fails characteristically by resolving one axis while ignoring the priority constraints on the others. We present a *deterministic agentic workflow* in contrast to self-planning agents: the control flow is fixed, language model calls are confined to narrow stages, and reflection and verification are retained as local mechanisms. This design yields interpretability by construction--each decision is decomposed into stage-wise structured outputs with verbatim citation of the chapter or section notes that bear on it. The architecture combines offline knowledge-engineering of the Chinese HS tariff with an online six-stage pipeline. Evaluated on HSCodeComp at the six-digit level, the workflow reaches 75.0% top-1 and 91.5% top-3 at four digits, and 64.2% top-1 and 78.3% top-3 at six digits with Qwen3.6-plus; an open-weight Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 backbone in non-thinking mode achieves 84.2% four-digit and 77.4% six-digit top-1 agreement with the frontier model. A two-stage manual audit of 226 six-digit disagreements suggests that a non-trivial fraction of HSCodeComp ground-truth labels may deviate from HS general rules; full adjudication records are released in the appendix as preliminary findings for community review.

</details>


### [123] [Small, Private Language Models as Teammates for Educational Assessment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15015)
*Chris Davis Jaldi,Anmol Saini,Shan Zhang,Noah Schroeder,Cogan Shimizu,Eleni Ilkou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了大语言模型（LLMs）和小语言模型（SLMs）在评估问题设计上的表现，发现SLMs在关键教学维度上达到竞争性表现且支持本地隐私部署，但模型评估相对于专家评审存在系统性的不一致性和偏差，说明语言模型应作为有限助手使用，需要人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究利用LLMs设计符合布鲁姆分类学的评估问题，但存在评估方法主观、主要关注专有模型、缺乏对生成/评估/部署约束的系统性检验等问题；同时SLMs作为保护隐私和资源的本地替代方案，其在评估任务中的有效性尚未充分探索。

Method: 系统比较LLMs和SLMs在评估问题设计上的表现；使用可复现的、基于教学法的指标评估跨布鲁姆分类学层次的生成质量；通过分析信度和一致性模式，对比基于模型的评判与专家评审。

Result: SLMs在关键教学驱动的质量维度上取得竞争性表现，同时支持本地、隐私敏感的部署；但模型评估相对于专家评分存在系统性的不一致和偏差。

Conclusion: 语言模型应作为评估工作流中的有限助手（bounded assistants），强调人机协作（Human-in-the-Loop）的必要性；推动了自动化教育问题生成领域对质量、可靠性和部署权衡的研究。

Abstract: Generative AI increasingly supports educational design tasks, e.g., through Large Language Models (LLMs), demonstrating the capability to design assessment questions that are aligned with pedagogical frameworks (e.g., Bloom's taxonomy). However, they often rely on subjective or limited evaluation methods; focus primarily on proprietary models; or rarely systematically examine generation, evaluation, or deployment constraints in real educational settings. Meanwhile, Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as local alternatives that better address privacy and resource limitations; yet their effectiveness for assessment tasks remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically compare LLMs and SLMs for assessment question design; evaluate generation quality across Bloom's taxonomy levels using reproducible, pedagogically grounded metrics; and further assess model-based judging against expert-informed evaluation by analyzing reliability and agreement patterns. Results show that SLMs achieve competitive performance across key pedagogically motivated quality dimensions while enabling local, privacy-sensitive deployment. However, model-based evaluations also exhibit systematic inconsistencies and bias relative to expert ratings. These findings provide evidence to posit language models as bounded assistants in assessment workflows; underscore the necessity of Human-in-the-Loop; and advance the automated educational question generation field by examining quality, reliability, and deployment-aware trade-offs.

</details>


### [124] [Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040)
*Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Xiao Yu,Rui Yang,Tao Ge,Alessandrio Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Yelong Shen,Pengcheng He,Tong Zhang,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchard是一个开源框架，通过轻量级环境服务Orchard Env实现可扩展的智能体建模。在三个领域（编码、GUI操作、个人助手）上，使用少量蒸馏数据和创新训练方法（信用分配SFT、平衡自适应 rollout RL）取得了开源模型最优或极具竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体建模投入巨大，但开放研究受限于基础设施和训练差距：高性能系统多依赖专有代码库/模型/服务，而开源框架侧重于编排和评估而非可扩展的智能体训练。

Method: 提出Orchard框架，核心是Orchard Env轻量级环境服务，提供沙箱生命周期管理的可复用原语。在此基础上构建三种智能体建模方案：Orchard-SWE（从MiniMax-M2.5和Qwen3.5-397B蒸馏107K轨迹，引入信用分配SFT从未解决轨迹的有效片段学习，采用平衡自适应 rollout进行RL）；Orchard-GUI（仅用0.4K蒸馏轨迹和2.2K开放任务训练4B视觉语言智能体）；Orchard-Claw（仅用0.2K合成任务训练个人助手智能体）。

Result: Orchard-SWE在SWE-bench Verified上达到64.3%（SFT后）和67.5%（SFT+RL后），创下同规模开源模型SOTA；Orchard-GUI在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web、DeepShop上分别达到74.1%、67.0%和64.0%成功率，为最强开源模型且与专有系统竞争；Orchard-Claw在Claw-Eval上达到59.6% pass@3，搭配更强ZeroClaw框架达73.9%。

Conclusion: 轻量级、开放、与框架无关的环境层能够跨领域实现可复用的智能体数据、训练方法和评估。

Abstract: Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Individual Intelligence: Surveying Collaboration, Failure Attribution, and Self-Evolution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14892)
*Shihao Qi,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wei Guo,Xiao Huang,Zhitao Gao,Jianhao Deng,Jun Liu,Lingling Zhang,Bifan Wei,Boqian Yang,Pinghui Wang,Jianwen Sun,Jing Tao,Yaqiang Wu,Hui Liu,Yu Yao,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述提出了LIFE框架，系统梳理了多智能体系统从能力构建、集成协作、故障归因到自主进化的四个因果关联阶段，揭示了错误传播与结构自改进的挑战，并提出了跨阶段的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别覆盖了单智能体能力、多智能体协作或智能体自我进化，但忽略了这些环节之间的因果依赖关系，尤其缺乏对错误跨智能体跨轮次传播及其转化为结构性自改进的机制性理解。

Method: 采用LIFE四阶段框架（能力构建→集成协作→故障归因→自主进化）进行统一综述，对每个阶段提供系统化分类法，并形式化表征相邻阶段之间的依赖与约束关系。

Result: 揭示了多智能体系统中错误传播的放大风险、各阶段间的因果依赖关系，以及当前在阶段边界上的开放性挑战。

Conclusion: 提出跨阶段研究议程，推动闭环多智能体系统实现持续故障诊断、结构重组和行为优化，朝向更自组织的集体智能方向演进。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, yet remain limited when tasks require sustained coordination across roles, tools, and environments. Multi-agent systems address this through structured collaboration among specialized agents, but tighter coordination also amplifies a less explored risk: errors can propagate across agents and interaction rounds, producing failures that are difficult to diagnose and rarely translate into structural self-improvement. Existing surveys cover individual agent capabilities, multi-agent collaboration, or agent self-evolution separately, leaving the causal dependencies among them unexamined. This survey provides a unified review organized around four causally linked stages, which we term the LIFE progression: Lay the capability foundation, Integrate agents through collaboration, Find faults through attribution, and Evolve through autonomous self-improvement. For each stage, we provide systematic taxonomies and formally characterize the dependencies between adjacent stages, revealing how each stage both depends on and constrains the next. Beyond synthesizing existing work, we identify open challenges at stage boundaries and propose a cross-stage research agenda for closed-loop multi-agent systems capable of continuously diagnosing failures, reorganizing structures, and refining agent behaviors, extending current coordination frameworks toward more self-organizing forms of collective intelligence. By bridging these previously fragmented research threads, this survey aims to offer both a systematic reference and a conceptual roadmap toward autonomous, self-improving multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [126] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST是一个基于案例驱动的框架，通过提取历史执行轨迹中的复杂度特征和失败模式，结合细粒度奖励设计与自适应推理策略，在强化学习框架下提升大语言模型的工具使用可靠性和结构有效性，在BFCLv2和ToolBench上实现了最高5.85%的执行准确率提升和26%的推理长度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在工具使用时，需要平衡适当的推理深度与严格的结构有效性，但现有方法在可靠执行方面存在不足，尤其是结构层面的错误频发。

Method: 提出CAST框架，采用案例驱动视角，将历史执行轨迹视为结构化案例，从中提取复杂度信号以预估最优推理策略，同时提取失败信号以映射可能的结构故障。这些知识被转化为细粒度奖励设计和自适应推理机制，使模型在强化学习过程中自主内化案例驱动的策略。

Result: 在BFCLv2和ToolBench上，CAST实现了最高5.85%的执行准确率提升，平均推理长度减少26%，显著降低了高影响的结构性错误。

Conclusion: 历史执行案例能够提供可复用的自适应知识，用于实现校准后的工具使用，证明了案例驱动方法在提升工具调用可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测条件下基于视觉观测的过去时间信号时态逻辑(ptSTL)的认证运行时监控。提出了一种可复用的监控框架，通过语义基（时态原子的鲁棒性得分向量）作为最小预测目标，结合单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口，实现单次共形校准即可认证整个公式片段。同时提出滚动预测监控器，在短时域上更紧致，而语义基监控器在长时域上紧致性提升4倍。在Waymo真实数据上验证了覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监控方法需要为每个公式重新训练，缺乏可复用性；且需要从图像中推断安全相关量并提供有限样本保证。

Method: 提出语义基（semantic basis）作为最小预测目标，利用单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口，通过确定性的解析树解码器评估任意公式，单次共形校准即可认证整个公式片段。同时提出滚动预测监控器在线重构时态历史。

Result: 滚动监控器在短时域上认证边界更紧致，语义基监控器在长时域上紧致性提升4倍。在Waymo数据上两种监控器均满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 提出的可复用监控框架有效解决了部分可观测条件下ptSTL的认证运行时监控问题，无需为每个公式重新训练，且提供了有限样本覆盖保证。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [128] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督框架Joint-VAE，通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器与解码器之间自适应平衡离散和连续潜变量，以分离dMRI结构连接组中的采集效应与生物变异。在N=7,416的大规模数据集上（涵盖2-102岁、13个研究、25种采集参数组合），该方法在捕获采集相关变异（ARI=0.53）上优于标准VAE、PCA+k-means及基于损失退火的混合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据跨站点、扫描仪和协议的采集差异引入变异性，传统降维方法将所有变异视为连续，导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜空间；现有混合潜空间模型需手动调节容量以让离散分量捕获目标变异，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE，在编码器输出后、解码器前引入架构退火（architectural annealing），自适应平衡离散与连续潜变量，无需手动调节容量。数据集包含7,416个结构连接组（5,900认知正常、877轻度认知障碍、639阿尔茨海默病），对比标准VAE、PCA+k-means和基于损失退火的混合模型。

Result: 架构退火方法在站点学习上ARI=0.53（p<0.05），优于所有基线方法。Joint-VAE恢复的聚类与扫描仪和协议差异对齐。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜空间结合架构而非损失退火，能有效无监督地捕获dMRI中的采集变异性，为分离采集效应与生物学变异提供了有用机制。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [129] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于联邦学习的框架（基于Sherpa.ai平台），使得分布在多个机构的私有且非IID的数据能够在不共享原始数据的情况下，通过参数高效微调（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3）联合微调大语言模型。在医疗和金融领域的四个问答/分类数据集上的实验表明，联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立学习；从绿色AI角度，QLoRA和IA3在精度损失有限的情况下提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的进一步发展需要利用分布在机构间的私有数据（如医疗、金融领域），但这些数据受隐私、法规和组织壁垒限制无法共享，且呈现非独立同分布（non-IID）特征。现有方法无法在保护隐私的前提下有效利用这些分散的、异构的私有数据。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建联邦微调框架，节点在不交换私有数据的情况下联合微调共享LLM。采用三种参数高效微调策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3），在非IID设置下模拟机构数据异构性，在医疗（MedQA、MedMCQA）和金融（FPB、FiQA-SA）四个数据集上进行封闭式问答和分类任务评估。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单机构独立学习。在绿色AI视角下，QLoRA和IA3在保持有限精度损失的同时提升了训练效率，验证了联邦PEFT作为数据不可共享场景下LLM适配的可行方案。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）是一种可行的、隐私保护的LLM适配方法，能够有效利用分散的私有数据，且QLoRA和IA3在效率与精度之间取得了良好平衡，支持绿色AI理念。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [130] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自进化记忆架构，不仅存储内容会演化，检索机制（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）也会通过LLM驱动的诊断模块进行闭环优化，在LoCoMo和MemBench上显著超越基线，且发现的配置具有跨基准迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统仅更新存储内容，而检索基础设施（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）在部署后固定不变。这使得记忆系统无法真正自适应，需要实现知识内容与检索机制的双层协同进化。

Method: EvolveMem将完整检索配置建模为结构化动作空间，由LLM驱动的诊断模块读取每个问题的失败日志、识别根因并提出配置调整方案；配备带自动回滚（revert-on-regression）和停滞探索（explore-on-stagnation）机制的元分析器，形成闭环自进化过程，实现架构自身的AutoResearch。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem超越最强基线25.7%（相对提升），相比最小基线提升78.0%；在MemBench上超越最强基线18.9%。进化出的配置可跨基准正向迁移，而非灾难性迁移。

Conclusion: 自进化过程捕捉到了通用的检索原则而非基准特定的启发式策略，证明了检索机制与存储内容协同进化的有效性，且自动收敛发现了原始动作空间中不存在的全新配置维度。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [131] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标重述为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中权重方案与嵌入几何结构之间的精确关系，证明了权重选择决定了对比学习目标是否几何可实现、退化或不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习的嵌入几何结构尚未被充分理解，缺乏理论框架来精确描述不同权重方案下最优嵌入的特征。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题，通过理论分析推导出监督和弱监督目标下最优嵌入的精确表征。

Result: 平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单纯形几何，而类别不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度；Soft SupCon无论类别是否平衡均保持正则单纯形；y-Aware CL在连续标签设置下一般无法达到其熵最优，除非标签位于超球面上；欧几里得-欧几里得权重和X-CLR存在唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 权重方案的选择决定了对比学习的几何可实现性，为设计对比学习目标提供了原理性框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [132] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Collider-Bench是一个评估LLM智能体能否仅凭公开论文和开源软件复现大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析的基准。测试表明，目前尚无通用编码智能体能够稳定超越物理学家参与的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别，特别是LHC分析因公开工具链与实际协作软件存在差距、论文省略实现细节而难以复现。

Method: 要求智能体将已发表分析转化为可执行的模拟-筛选流水线，提交指定信号区间的碰撞事件产额预测；使用标准直方图指标提供连续保真度评分，并报告计算成本；利用LLM法官评估代码和会话痕迹以捕捉幻觉、编造等故障模式。

Result: 平均而言，没有通用编码智能体能够可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

Conclusion: 当前通用LLM编码智能体在复现LHC分析方面尚无法超越人类物理学家，该基准揭示了AI在科学复现任务中的局限。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [133] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间接地先验分布，替代扩散和流匹配模型中标准的高斯源分布，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率，并改善了强化学习的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别出生成式机器人控制策略中源分布这一被忽视但重要的设计维度，并探索更优的替代方案。

Method: 将扩散/流匹配模型的标准高斯源分布替换为WarmPrior——基于近期可用动作历史构建的简单时间接地先验分布。

Result: 1) 机器人操作任务的成功率持续提升；2) 概率路径显著变得更直，与Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果类似；3) 在先验空间强化学习中改善了探索分布，提升了样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要且尚未充分探索的设计轴，WarmPrior是一种简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能源核算框架，用于测量知识蒸馏管道的完整计算成本（包括教师端数据生成、logit缓存、评估等被忽略的能耗），并通过实验构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了经典蒸馏和合成数据微调两种方法的真实能源与排放成本，最终给出在能源约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的设计规则，并开源了标准化测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在宣传蒸馏可降低模型成本时，通常忽略了教师端数据生成、logit缓存、评估等完整端到端的能源和资源成本，导致对蒸馏真实能耗的低估，缺乏系统性的全流程能源核算框架。

Method: 提出分阶段GPU功耗追踪的能源核算框架，对两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调）进行实验，分离并记录不同阶段的能耗，构建能量-质量帕累托前沿，分析被忽略的隐藏成本。

Result: 通过实验测量揭示了蒸馏管道中被忽略的教师端能耗成本，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，提供了在能源和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的设计规则。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的真实能耗远高于以往认知，教师端开销不可忽视；所提出的核算框架和开源工具为可重复、可比较的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [135] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个基于TabPFN扩展的表格数据基础模型，通过纯合成数据预训练，支持百万级训练样本，在标准基准上显著优于所有现有模型，同时推理速度提升20倍，并引入测试时计算缩放、支持关系数据和时间序列等新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据支撑了科学和工业中的大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN已引领该模态的基础模型革命。本文旨在基于用户反馈，将TabPFN扩展到百万级训练数据规模，同时大幅降低训练和推理时间，并拓展到时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据等更广泛的场景。

Method: 仅在合成数据上从头预训练；引入测试时计算缩放（test-time compute scaling）；采用减少KV缓存和行分块（row-chunking）策略支持百万行数据；针对不同模态训练专用检查点（如TabPFN-TS-3用于时间序列）。

Result: 在TabArena基准上前向传播显著优于所有调参和集成的基线模型，在帕累托前沿上占优；在多样数据集上，多类别数据集排名第一，在百万行/200特征数据集上优于8小时调优的梯度提升树基线；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上超越所有非TabPFN模型200+ Elo，在大数据子集上达420 Elo，比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍；在RelBenchV1上达到新SOTA，在TabSTAR上通过TabPFN-3-Plus达SOTA；TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二；SHAP值计算加速高达120倍；整体速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过大规模合成数据预训练和架构优化，在表格数据预测任务上全面超越现有方法，同时显著提升速度和多模态扩展能力，证明了纯合成数据预训练和测试时计算缩放的有效性。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [136] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏MoE层中专家合并时存在一个被现有方法忽视的"不可约三元环"障碍，该障碍本质上是2-复形上单纯拉普拉斯算子的调和核。作者提出HodgeCover方法，通过霍奇分解识别并覆盖调和关键边和三元组关键三角形，在激进压缩场景下达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练压缩方法仅基于成对信号（如KL散度）对专家进行合并排名，但存在一个结构性盲点：三个专家可能两两兼容但三者构成不可约环，导致任何成对评分都无法判断三元组是否可联合合并。这一数学障碍阻碍了现有压缩器的进一步提升。

Method: 将专家视为顶点，专家间的KL合并障碍为边，三元组障碍为面，构成2-复形；通过霍奇分解将边障碍信号分解，精确分离出调和核（即三元环障碍）。提出HodgeCover：贪心覆盖调和关键边和三元组关键三角形；混合变体进一步结合权重剪枝。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上，HodgeCover在专家缩减维度匹配SOTA基线，在混合压缩（专家缩减+权重剪枝）的激进前沿维度领先，并且独特地在所有四个霍奇分量上保持了均衡的保留质量。

Conclusion: 暴露MoE结构的调和核能够改变最优压缩器的选择，尤其是在最关键的激进压缩场景下。这一数学框架为设计更好的无训练MoE压缩方法提供了新视角。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [137] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了离散扩散模型（均匀和吸收/掩码扩散）的去噪目标在最后步骤中会自然分离出层次结构：先学习数据支撑（如语法有效句子的结构），后学习数据频率（相对概率分布）。均匀扩散呈现三分法（有效性改进/保持/恶化），而吸收扩散主要集中于有效性改进。实验验证了支撑定位先于频率排序，且两种扩散机制表现出预测的速率分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中越来越有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程尚不清楚，特别是模型如何从数据分布中逐步学习不同层次的结构信息。

Method: 通过理论分析证明在均匀和吸收扩散的小噪声区域（最后去噪步骤），每个单token反向编辑可分解为领先尺度（是否朝向数据支撑）和精细系数（确定同一尺度内的相对概率）。并通过掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务实验验证理论预测。

Result: 支撑定位（如语法有效性）在训练中比频率排序更早出现；均匀扩散展现出有效性改进、保持和恶化的三分法，而吸收扩散主要产生有效性改进的编辑；两种机制的速率分离与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型学习数据支撑先于学习数据频率，这种层次化学习机制由扩散类型（均匀vs吸收）决定，为理解离散扩散模型的学习动力学提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [138] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave提出了一种面向IoT传感信号的动态分词框架，利用小波层次分解识别语义事件边界，自适应压缩冗余区间，在五个真实数据集上准确率提升最高12%，输入token长度减少最高75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统采集的传感信号具有非平稳、多尺度特性，标准分词技术难以有效处理此类信号，亟需一种能对齐内在时间结构与物理事件的紧凑输入表征方法。

Method: 基于小波层次分解，识别与底层语义事件对应的时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间同时保持时间连贯性，构建动态分词框架Dywave。

Result: 在活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测等五个真实IoT数据集上，Dywave相比现有方法准确率提升最高12%，主流序列模型输入token长度减少最高75%，对领域偏移和可变序列长度表现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave通过动态分词有效解决了IoT传感信号的表征难题，兼顾了准确率与计算效率，且具备良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [139] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高更新-数据比(UTD)下自预测学习(SPL)中的表征过拟合问题，提出基于冗余减少的鲁棒表示方法(R2R2)，通过非中心化目标解决标准零中心化与SPL谱性质的冲突，在11个连续控制任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的真实机器人等强化学习场景中，高频率的数据复用虽能提升效率但会导致过拟合。现有工作主要关注评论家偏差问题，而高UTD比率下自预测学习中的表征层不稳定性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出R2R2正则化方法：理论上识别出标准零中心化与SPL谱性质存在冲突，据此设计了非中心化目标函数；在SPL原生算法TD7上验证，并将SPL模块集成到原本不含SPL的最先进算法SimbaV2中，形成SimbaV2-SPL。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，R2R2有效缓解过拟合；在UTD比率为20时，提升TD7约22%，并在SimbaV2-SPL基础上带来额外增益，SimbaV2-SPL本身即达到新的最优结果。

Conclusion: R2R2是一种有效的表征级正则化方法，与现有评论家偏置方面的改进正交，可作为高数据效率强化学习的通用模块。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [140] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SP-KV是一种自剪枝键值注意力机制，通过轻量级效用预测器动态评估KV对的未来价值，在保留局部窗口的同时，仅将高效用预测值的旧KV对存入长期缓存，实现3-10倍缓存压缩且几乎不损失性能，并揭示出可指导混合注意力架构设计的层/头特异性稀疏模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理越来越长的序列，Transformer架构中KV缓存的内存占用和带宽成为高效文本生成的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV)，包含一个轻量级效用预测器为每个KV对打分，近期KV始终通过局部窗口可用，而旧KV对仅当预测效用超过阈值时才写入缓存并参与全局注意力。LLM与效用预测器通过下一词预测损失联合端到端训练，从预训练检查点微调适配。

Result: SP-KV实现动态稀疏化，通常将KV缓存大小压缩3-10倍，长序列压缩比更高；显著改善内存使用和解码速度，验证损失和下游任务性能几乎没有下降；揭示了结构化的层特异性和头特异性稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV不仅能有效减少KV缓存，其揭示的稀疏模式还可用于指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [141] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMemSafe，一种基于可靠性门控的CTTA方法，通过检测冻结源模型的预测熵来动态调整源锚定强度，在源模型失效时自动退化为无源备用方案，在CCC-Hard等极端分布漂移场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源锚定CTTA方法在源模型因分布漂移而失效时（如ResNet-50在CCC-Hard上准确率降至1.3%），仍以相同强度锚定源模型，导致"盲目锚定"（blind anchoring）失败模式。

Method: RMemSafe是ROID的扩展，引入可靠性门控机制：利用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵作为门控信号，当源后验接近均匀分布时关闭门控，移除源锚定和一致性滤波，目标函数退化为无源备用方案（ROID基础损失+边际校准）。

Result: 在9个匹配分割的连续损坏测试单元中，RMemSafe+ASR在8个上取得最低错误率，在所有9个上是最优的重置基方法；相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点；源退化扫描实验显示损伤斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍。

Conclusion: 熵门控能有效检测高熵源崩溃（而非自信但错误低熵源），实现源锚定的优雅退化，解决了盲目锚定问题。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration.
